# Gravitee Documentation

## Home

- [Gravitee Documentation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-documentation.md): Welcome to Gravitee!

## Gravitee Gamma

- [Gravitee Gamma](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/readme.md): Gravitee Gamma unifies API Management, Event Stream Management, and Agent Management on one platform. Learn what Gamma offers and get started.
- [Overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/overview.md): Gravitee Gamma governs API, event stream, and AI agent traffic from one platform. Learn how its modules, gateways, and shared catalog fit together.
- [Release Notes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/gamma-release-notes.md): Everything in the first Gravitee Gamma release, across Agent, API, Authorization, Edge, and Event Stream Management. Browse the highlights.
- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started.md): Choose the Gamma getting-started path that fits you, whether you are new to Gravitee or activating Gamma on an existing installation. Start here.
- [Get started for new users](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-new-users.md): New to Gravitee and want to run Gamma? Choose between Gravitee Next-Gen Cloud and installing it yourself. Compare the two paths and pick one.
- [Fully self-hosted installation with Vanilla Kubernetes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-new-users/fully-self-hosted-with-vanilla-kubernetes.md): Run a full Gamma platform yourself on Kubernetes with the Gravitee Helm chart. Choose the guide for your cluster and the ingress it needs.
- [Fully self-hosted installation with Docker](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-new-users/fully-self-hosted-with-docker.md): Run a full Gamma platform yourself with Docker, bringing up the consoles, Gateway, and datastores on one host. Choose the Docker guide that fits.
- [Get started for existing users](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-existing-users.md): Already running Gravitee? Activate Gamma on your existing installation instead of installing from scratch. Choose the path for how you run it.
- [Activate Gamma with Docker Compose](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-existing-users/activate-gamma-with-docker-compose.md): Activate Gamma on an existing Docker Compose installation of Gravitee by moving to the Gamma build. Follow the steps to enable and verify it.
- [Activate Gamma with Kubernetes (Helm)](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/get-started/get-started-for-existing-users/activate-gamma-with-kubernetes-helm.md): Activate Gamma on an existing Kubernetes deployment of Gravitee with the Helm chart. Follow the steps to move to the Gamma build and enable it.
- [Install](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install.md): Choose between a self-hosted Gamma installation, where you run everything, and a hybrid one, where Gravitee hosts the control plane. Pick your model.
- [Self-hosted installation guides](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides.md): A self-hosted Gamma installation runs every component in your own infrastructure. Choose a Docker or Kubernetes deployment method to begin.
- [Docker](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/docker.md): Every Gamma component on one host, from MongoDB and Elasticsearch to the consoles. Compare the Docker Compose and Docker CLI methods before you start.
- [Docker Compose](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/docker/docker-compose.md): Install a fully self-hosted Gamma platform on one host with Docker Compose. Follow the steps to write the compose file and add a license key.
- [Docker CLI](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/docker/docker-cli.md): Install a fully self-hosted Gamma platform on one host with the Docker CLI. Follow the steps to run the datastores, Gateway, and consoles.
- [Kubernetes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/kubernetes.md): Run the full Gamma stack on Kubernetes with the Gravitee Helm chart, datastores included. Pick the guide that matches the cluster you operate.
- [Vanilla Kubernetes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/kubernetes/vanilla-kubernetes.md): Install a fully self-hosted Gamma platform on a vanilla Kubernetes cluster. Follow the steps to deploy every component with the Helm chart.
- [AWS EKS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/kubernetes/aws-eks.md): Run the full Gamma stack on AWS EKS, with the load balancer controller plus the MongoDB and Elasticsearch datastores. Follow the steps to deploy it.
- [Azure AKS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/kubernetes/azure-aks.md): Run the full Gamma stack on Azure AKS behind an NGINX ingress controller. Follow the steps to create the namespace and install every component.
- [OpenShift](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/self-hosted-installation-guides/kubernetes/openshift.md): Install a fully self-hosted Gamma platform on OpenShift, with MongoDB and Elasticsearch. Follow the steps to deploy it with the Helm chart.
- [Hybrid installation guides](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides.md): In a hybrid Gamma deployment, Gravitee hosts the control plane and you run the Gateway. Choose a Docker or Kubernetes deployment method.
- [Docker](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/docker.md): A hybrid Gateway on one host talks to a Gravitee Cloud control plane. Compare the Docker Compose and Docker CLI methods and what each one runs.
- [Docker Compose](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/docker/docker-compose.md): Define a hybrid Gamma Gateway in a compose file and an env file, then bring it up in one command. Follow the steps to write and run them.
- [Docker CLI](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/docker/docker-cli.md): Start a hybrid Gamma Gateway container by container with the Docker CLI, creating the network and Redis first. Follow the steps to run it.
- [Kubernetes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/kubernetes.md): Deploy a hybrid Gamma Gateway with the Gravitee Helm chart on EKS, AKS, OpenShift, or vanilla Kubernetes. Choose the guide for your cluster.
- [Vanilla Kubernetes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/kubernetes/vanilla-kubernetes.md): Install a hybrid Gamma Gateway on a vanilla Kubernetes cluster with the Gravitee Helm chart. Follow the steps to deploy the Gateway and Redis.
- [AWS EKS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/kubernetes/aws-eks.md): Install a hybrid Gamma Gateway on AWS EKS, configuring the load balancer controller for Auto Mode or standard clusters. Follow the steps to deploy.
- [Azure AKS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/kubernetes/azure-aks.md): Install a hybrid Gamma Gateway on Azure AKS behind an NGINX ingress controller. Follow the steps to create the namespace and deploy the chart.
- [OpenShift](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/install/hybrid-installation-guides/kubernetes/openshift.md): Install a hybrid Gamma Gateway on OpenShift, from creating the project to running Redis. Follow the steps to deploy it with the Helm chart.
- [Overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/applications.md): The Overview group of the Platform Management sidebar holds the environment's Applications. Open it to work with the consumer applications.
- [Manage applications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/applications/manage-applications.md): View, create, and manage the consumer applications that subscribe to your API plans. Follow the steps to work with them on the Applications page.
- [APIs & Assets](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/apis-and-assets.md): The APIs & Assets group of the Platform Management sidebar holds the environment's Metadata and Dictionaries. Choose the one you want to manage.
- [Manage environment metadata](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/apis-and-assets/manage-environment-metadata.md): The Metadata page in the Gamma console holds the key-value entries that every API in the selected environment inherits. Add, edit, search, and delete them.
- [Manage dictionaries](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/apis-and-assets/manage-dictionaries.md): Create, deploy, and manage the dictionaries that provide environment-scoped lookup data to API policies from the Dictionaries page in the Gamma console.
- [System & Security](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/system-and-security.md): The System & Security group of the Platform Management sidebar holds the environment's Access Management connection. Open it to configure the connection.
- [Configure Access Management](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/system-and-security/configure-access-management.md): Connect the Gamma console to a Gravitee Access Management instance so you can sync its domains. Follow the steps to configure the connection.
- [Configure OpenTelemetry tracing and logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/system-and-security/configure-opentelemetry-tracing-and-logs.md): The Gamma Trace Explorer reads from an OpenTelemetry pipeline you run alongside Gravitee. Follow the steps to export, read, and collect traces.
- [Configure OpenAPI viewer](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/platform-management/system-and-security/configure-openapi-viewer.md): Choose which viewer the Gamma console and Developer Portal use to render OpenAPI specifications. Follow the steps to change the default viewer.
- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/get-started.md): API Management in Gamma, from the overview of its key concepts to the quickstart that builds your first API proxy. Start with the page you need.
- [API Management overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/get-started/api-management-overview.md): API Management governs REST, GraphQL, gRPC, and WebSocket traffic through API proxies. Learn what it does and how it fits the Gamma platform.
- [Create your first API](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/get-started/create-your-first-api.md): Create a keyless API proxy in the Gamma console, deploy it to the API Gateway, and verify it with a test request. Follow the quickstart to build one.
- [Migrate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/migrate.md): Migrate an API estate to Gravitee API Management from Kong, Apigee, MuleSoft, Azure, or another gateway. Start by planning the migration.
- [Plan a gateway migration](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/migrate/plan-a-gateway-migration.md): Plan a gateway migration to Gravitee API Management from Kong, Apigee, MuleSoft, or Azure. Learn how the method works and what it refuses to convert.
- [Migrate an API estate to Gravitee](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/migrate/migrate-an-api-estate-to-gravitee.md): Migrate an API estate from a legacy gateway to Gravitee API Management with the migration agent skill. Follow the steps to convert and reconcile it.
- [Source gateway reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/migrate/source-gateway-reference.md): Source gateway migration reference for Gravitee API Management, covering Management API paths, Expression Language syntax, and credential export.
- [Deploy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/deploy.md): What to expect from the API Management Gateway under load before you run APIs on it.
- [Gateway benchmarks](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/deploy/gateway-benchmarks.md): Release-over-release performance results for the API Management Gateway on sync proxy APIs, with the scenarios and controlled environment used to produce them.
- [Manage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage.md): Open the Manage group of the API Management sidebar and choose API Proxies or API Products.
- [API Proxies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies.md): Create an API proxy, then configure it from the groups of its own sidebar. Choose the group that holds the setting you need.
- [Create an API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/create-an-api-proxy.md): Create an API proxy in the Gamma console with the from-scratch wizard or a quick-start template. Follow the steps through each wizard option.
- [Configure your API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/configure-your-api-proxy.md): Refine an API proxy from the detail workspace, which groups configuration into seven sidebar sections. Start with the setting you need to change.
- [General](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general.md): The General group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Overview, General, API Properties, Resources, Notifications, API Score, Response Templates, and CORS pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage general settings](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/manage-general-settings.md): Edit the name, version, metadata, and images of an API proxy, and start, stop, or delete it. Follow the steps on the General page to update them.
- [Configure API properties](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-api-properties.md): Define static or dynamic key/value properties that policies read at runtime through the Expression Language. Follow the steps to add and encrypt them.
- [Configure API resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-api-resources.md): Create API-level resources such as caches and OAuth providers that this API's policies reference at runtime. Follow the steps to add and manage them.
- [Configure notifications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-notifications.md): Send console, email, or webhook alerts when API events occur, such as a deployment or a subscription change. Follow the steps to add a notification.
- [Configure CORS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-cors.md): Let browsers on other origins call your API by enabling cross-origin resource sharing and setting the allowed origins. Follow the steps to configure it.
- [Gateway](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway.md): The Gateway group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Entrypoints, Endpoints, Failover, Health Check Dashboard, and Reporter Settings pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure entrypoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-entrypoints.md): Change the context paths consumers use to reach an API proxy, or switch to virtual hosts. Follow the steps on the Entrypoints page to update them.
- [Configure endpoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-backend-security.md): Endpoint groups define where the API Gateway routes requests, with load balancing, timeouts, and TLS. Follow the steps to create a group and add endpoints.
- [Configure failover](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-failover.md): Turn on automatic retries with a circuit breaker so a slow or failing backend doesn't take your API down. Follow the steps to tune the thresholds.
- [Monitor endpoint health](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/monitor-endpoint-health.md): Track backend endpoint availability, response times, and failed health checks for an API. Follow the steps to open the Health Check Dashboard.
- [Configure logging and tracing](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-logging-and-tracing.md): Control which request and response data an API proxy reports to logs and analytics, and turn on OpenTelemetry tracing. Follow the steps to configure it.
- [Design](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design.md): The Design group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Policy Studio pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Apply security policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/apply-security-policies.md): Policies are rules the API Gateway evaluates on every request and response, on top of security plans. Learn how the policy chain runs and how to apply one.
- [Control how policy flows are matched to requests](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/configure-flow-execution.md): Choose whether the API Gateway runs every matching policy flow or only the closest match. Follow the steps to set the flow execution mode.
- [Reuse policies with shared policy groups](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/shared-policy-groups.md): A shared policy group configures a set of policies once and reuses them across APIs and flows. Follow the steps to attach one in the Policy Studio.
- [Consumer Access](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access.md): The Consumer Access group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Plans, Consumers, and Broadcasts pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Secure your API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/secure-your-api-proxy.md): Attach a Keyless, API Key, JWT, OAuth2, or mTLS plan to control how consumers authenticate to an API proxy. Follow the steps to add one and deploy it.
- [Establish consumer access](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/establish-consumer-access.md): Applications, subscriptions, and API keys control how consumers discover and authenticate with your API. Learn how to review and approve a request.
- [Broadcast messages to consumers](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/broadcast-messages-to-consumers.md): Send a one-way announcement to the consumers of an API proxy about a change, an update, or a maintenance window. Follow the steps to compose one.
- [Security](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/security.md): The Security group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Authorization and User Permissions pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage user permissions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/security/manage-user-permissions.md): Add direct members, attach groups, and transfer primary ownership of an API proxy. Follow the steps on the User Permissions page to manage access.
- [Monitor](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/monitoring.md): The Monitoring group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Alerts and Audit Logs pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Review audit logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/monitoring/review-audit-logs.md): Trace who changed what, and when, on an API proxy with its audit trail. Follow the steps to read and filter the events on the Audit Logs page.
- [Operate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations.md): The Operations group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Deployment pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations/configure-deployment.md): Choose which API Gateway instances load an API definition by assigning sharding tags. Follow the steps on the Deployment Configuration page.
- [Review deployment history](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations/review-deployment-history.md): Compare two deployed versions of an API definition and roll back to an earlier one. Follow the steps to inspect the history and restore a version.
- [API Products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products.md): Create an API Product, then configure it from the groups of its own sidebar. Choose the group that holds the setting you need.
- [Create API Products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/api-products.md): API Products bundle multiple API proxies into one consumer-facing product with shared plans. Learn when to use them and how to create one.
- [Configure API products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/configure-your-api-product.md): Attach APIs, create plans, manage consumers and team access, and choose where an API Product deploys. Start with the area you need to configure.
- [General](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/general.md): The General group of an API Product's sidebar holds the Overview, General, and APIs pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage product APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/general/manage-product-apis.md): Attach and detach API proxies from an API Product so subscribers reach every API it bundles. Follow the steps to manage them on the APIs tab.
- [Security](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/security.md): The Security group of an API Product's sidebar holds the User Permissions pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage members and ownership](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/security/manage-members-and-ownership.md): Add members, attach groups, change roles, and transfer ownership of an API product. Follow the steps to manage access from the User Permissions tab.
- [Operate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/operations.md): The Operations group of an API Product's sidebar holds the Deployment pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure product deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/operations/configure-product-deployment.md): Assign sharding tags to an API Product so only the API Gateway instances that advertise a matching tag load it. Follow the steps on the Deployment tab.
- [Manage API products with the Management API](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/manage-api-products-with-the-management-api.md): Create an API product, attach APIs, add and publish plans, and deploy it over the v2 Management API. Follow the full lifecycle with request examples.
- [API product configuration reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/manage/api-products/api-product-configuration-reference.md): Every API product property, the flag that decides which APIs can be bundled, and the plan types a product supports. Browse the full reference.
- [Observe](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/observability.md): The Observability group of the API Management sidebar holds the Dashboards, Logs, and Tracing pages. Open the view you need.
- [Dashboard and metrics](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/observability/api-dashboard.md): The Gamma homepage dashboard shows per-module metrics, quick actions, and guidance for empty states. Learn what each metric and quick action does.
- [Monitor API usage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/observability/monitor-api-usage.md): Track request volume, latency distribution, error rates, and top consumers for an API proxy. Follow the steps to open the observability dashboard.
- [View API logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/api-management/observability/view-api-logs.md): Inspect individual request logs and full request traces for an API proxy in the Trace Explorer. Follow the steps to open logs and traces.
- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/get-started.md): Event Stream Management in Gamma, from the product overview to the quickstarts for clusters, Kafka Services, and Virtual Clusters. Start where you need.
- [Event Stream Management overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/get-started/event-stream-management-overview.md): Event Stream Management governs Kafka clusters, event-driven data flows, and streaming infrastructure. Learn what it does and how it fits Gamma.
- [Register your first cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/get-started/register-your-first-cluster.md): Register your first Kafka cluster in the Gamma console and add its first connection. Follow the quickstart to create and deploy the cluster.
- [Create your first Kafka service](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/get-started/create-your-first-kafka-service.md): Create a governed Kafka Service in the Gamma console with a standalone endpoint and a keyless plan. Follow the quickstart to get one running.
- [Create your first Virtual Cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/get-started/create-your-first-virtual-cluster.md): Federate two Registered Clusters into a single unified endpoint with a Virtual Cluster. Follow the quickstart to compose and create one.
- [Manage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/manage.md): The Manage group of the Event Stream Management sidebar holds your registered clusters. Choose the page you need.
- [Register your Kafka clusters](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/manage/register-your-kafka-clusters.md): Register an existing Kafka cluster with Gamma so Kafka Services and Virtual Clusters can be built on it. Follow the steps to register and deploy one.
- [Build](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build.md): Create and configure governed Kafka Services and Virtual Clusters on top of your registered Kafka infrastructure. Start with the service you need.
- [Create a Kafka service with a registered cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build/create-a-kafka-service-with-a-registered-cluster.md): Create a Kafka Service bound to a connection on a registered Kafka cluster, with security plans and policies. Follow the steps in the creation wizard.
- [Create a Kafka service with a Virtual Cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build/create-a-kafka-service-with-a-virtual-cluster.md): Create a Kafka Service backed by a Virtual Cluster to govern a federated multi-cluster Kafka environment. Follow the steps to build it in the wizard.
- [Establish a Virtual Cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build/establish-a-virtual-cluster.md): Federate multiple Kafka backends into one endpoint by establishing a Virtual Cluster. Follow the steps to choose a topology and compose the backends.
- [Virtual Clusters overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build/kafka-virtual-clusters-overview.md): A Virtual Cluster presents several Kafka backends to clients as one endpoint. Learn the key concepts, Mesh mode, and the limitations that apply.
- [Virtual Cluster runtime behavior reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/event-stream-management/build/kafka-virtual-cluster-runtime-behavior-reference.md): What a deployed Virtual Cluster does on the Event Gateway, from lifecycle states to topic routing and consumer groups. Browse the full reference.
- [Overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/agent-management/overview.md): Agent Management governs LLM, MCP, and agent-to-agent traffic from one control plane. Find the use case you need and the guide that covers it.
- [LLM Proxy provider support](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/agent-management/overview/llm-proxy-provider-support.md): Which OpenAI features each LLM Proxy provider supports, how requests map to each native API, and the limits that apply. Browse the full reference.

- [Create your first user](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/get-started/create-your-first-user.md): Create a local principal in Authorization Management so the policy engine has an identity to evaluate. Follow the steps to add your first one.
- [Import your first resource from Agent catalog](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/get-started/import-your-first-resource-from-agent-catalog.md): Import a resource from the AI Catalog so every policy refers to one canonical Entity ID. Follow the quickstart to import your first item.
- [Create your first policy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/get-started/create-your-first-policy.md): Create and deploy your first authorization policy in the visual editor to get a permit rule enforced at runtime. Follow the steps to build one.
- [Policy Structure](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage.md): The Policy Structure group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the entities, actions, and schemas your policies are written against.
- [Manage principals](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/principals.md): Sync principals from Access Management, create local ones, and give them attributes and relationships. Start with how your identities arrive.
- [Sync principals from Access Management](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/principals/sync-principals-from-access-management.md): Synchronize users and groups from Gravitee Access Management so policies can reference your identity directory. Follow the steps to run a sync.
- [Create a local principal](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/principals/create-a-local-principal.md): Create a principal directly in Authorization Management when the identity is not synced or imported. Follow the steps on the Entities page.
- [Add attributes to principals](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/principals/add-attributes-to-principals.md): Attach custom metadata to a principal, such as department or device trust, so policies test more than identity. Follow the steps to add attributes.
- [Build principal relationships](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/principals/build-principal-relationships.md): Link principals into parent-child hierarchies so that one policy can match every user in a group. Follow the steps to set and edit parents.
- [Manage resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/resources.md): Import resources from the Catalog, create local ones, and give them attributes and relationships. Start with where your resources come from.
- [Import resources from Catalog](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/resources/import-resources-from-catalog.md): Import resource entities from the AI Catalog into Authorization Management across all three categories. Follow the steps to browse and select them.
- [Create a local resource](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/resources/create-a-local-resource.md): Create a resource entity for the internal applications, data assets, and custom tools the AI Catalog does not hold. Follow the steps to add one.
- [Add attributes to resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/resources/add-attributes-to-resources.md): Attach custom metadata to a resource, such as sensitivity or ownership, so policy conditions can test it. Follow the steps to add attributes.
- [Build resource relationships](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/resources/build-resource-relationships.md): Establish parent-child hierarchies between resources so one policy covers a server and all of its tools. Follow the steps to set parents.
- [Manage actions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/actions.md): Actions are the verbs your policies grant or forbid. Create one, and see how action Entity IDs are named and scoped. Start by creating an action.
- [Create an action](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/actions/create-an-action.md): Define the verbs your policies grant or forbid by creating an action entity that statements reference. Follow the steps on the Actions page.
- [Action naming and scope](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/actions/action-naming-and-scope.md): How action Entity IDs are structured and how the policy engine resolves them against policy types and resource scopes. Browse the naming reference.
- [Manage schemas](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/schemas.md): The schema declares the entity types, relationships, and actions your policies are written against. Start with how a schema is generated.
- [Schema generation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/schemas/schema-generation.md): The schema is the contract your policies are written against, declaring entity types, relationships, and actions. Learn how it is generated and read.
- [Edit your schema](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/schemas/edit-your-schema.md): Modify an existing schema to add entity types, attributes, relationships, or actions. Follow the steps to edit, validate, and save your changes.
- [Validate schema changes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/manage/schemas/validate-schema-changes.md): How the console validates a schema change before saving, and the errors to expect. Learn the validation pipeline and how to fix a failure.
- [Manage policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure.md): The Policy Management group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the policies for MCPs, AI models, APIs, A2A agents, and custom resources.
- [Create, update, and delete policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/create-update-delete-policies.md): Reference for the Authorization Management policy editor, covering how to create, name, build, deploy, and delete a policy. Browse the full reference.
- [MCP policy examples](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/mcp-policy-examples.md): Govern MCP servers, tools, and prompts with policy examples for group access, time windows, and device trust. Adapt an example to your own servers.
- [AI policy example](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/ai-policy-example.md): Control which teams reach which AI models with policy examples for token budgets, cost ceilings, and guardrails. Adapt an example to your own models.
- [API policy examples](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/api-policy-examples.md): Govern API proxies, endpoints, and data fields with policy examples for scopes, rate limits, and tenant isolation. Adapt an example to your own APIs.
- [Custom policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies.md): Custom policies govern resources outside the MCP, API, Agent, AI Model, and Event categories. Start with the overview, then create your first one.
- [Custom policies overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies/custom-policies-overview.md): Custom policies protect internal applications, data assets, and bespoke resources. Learn how they differ from service policies and when to use them.
- [Create a custom policy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies/create-a-custom-policy.md): Create an authorization policy for resources not routed through the MCP, API, Agent, AI Model, or Event categories. Follow the steps to build one.
- [PDP Gateways](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate.md): The PDP Gateways group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the gateways that evaluate your policies and how policies reach them.
- [Configure the Gravitee Gateway as a runtime](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate/configure-gravitee-gateway-as-runtime.md): Register a Gravitee Gateway as a Policy Decision Point so authorization decisions are evaluated at the wire level. Follow the steps to register one.
- [Configure a sidecar as runtime](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate/configure-sidecar-as-runtime.md): Deploy the Policy Decision Point as a sidecar beside your application for latency-sensitive or air-gapped environments. Follow the steps to deploy it.
- [Policy syncs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate/policy-syncs.md): How authorization policies travel from the control plane to your runtime gateways, and how long it takes. Learn to read and monitor sync status.
- [Analytics sent to the control plane](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate/analytics-sent-to-control-plane.md): The observability data that Policy Decision Point gateways send back to the Gravitee control plane. Learn what is collected and where it appears.
- [Authorization policy synchronization](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/authorization-management/evaluate/authz-gateway-sync.md): Gamma Authorization Management evaluates policies locally inside the API Gateway. Learn how a change in the console becomes enforceable.
- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/get-started.md): Edge Management in Gamma, starting with what the Edge Daemon does on employee devices and how it fits the platform. Start with the overview.
- [Edge Management overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/get-started/edge-management-overview.md): The Edge Daemon observes AI traffic on employee devices, enforces local policy, and forwards it to the AI Gateway. Learn how it routes traffic.
- [Connect](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/connect.md): Connect your edge environments and tools to the Gamma Edge Daemon, from the proxy APIs to fleet deployment. Start with the task you need.
- [Proxy API reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/connect/proxy-api-reference.md): The LLM Proxy and HTTP proxy APIs that receive the traffic an Edge Daemon forwards, and the plan they must expose. Browse the full reference.
- [Configure Edge Management](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/connect/configure-edge-management.md): Set the Gateway URLs, proxy routes, and shadow AI monitoring for Edge Management in one place. Follow the steps to create and save a configuration.
- [Configure Edge Ingress](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/connect/configure-edge-ingress.md): Expose the Edge Reactor port on Kubernetes so Edge Daemons on employee devices reach the AI Gateway. Follow the steps to enable ingress in Helm.
- [Configure Kandji to deploy the Edge Daemon](https://documentation.gravitee.io/gravitee-gamma/edge-management/connect/configure-kandji-daemon.md): Deploy the Edge Daemon across your macOS fleet with Kandji, using the script the console generates. Follow the steps to create the Custom App.

## API Management

- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/get-started.md): API Management in Gamma, from the overview of its key concepts to the quickstart that builds your first API proxy. Start with the page you need.
- [API Management overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/get-started/api-management-overview.md): API Management governs REST, GraphQL, gRPC, and WebSocket traffic through API proxies. Learn what it does and how it fits the Gamma platform.
- [Create your first API](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/get-started/create-your-first-api.md): Create a keyless API proxy in the Gamma console, deploy it to the API Gateway, and verify it with a test request. Follow the quickstart to build one.
- [Migrate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/migrate.md): Migrate an API estate to Gravitee API Management from Kong, Apigee, MuleSoft, Azure, or another gateway. Start by planning the migration.
- [Plan a gateway migration](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/migrate/plan-a-gateway-migration.md): Plan a gateway migration to Gravitee API Management from Kong, Apigee, MuleSoft, or Azure. Learn how the method works and what it refuses to convert.
- [Migrate an API estate to Gravitee](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/migrate/migrate-an-api-estate-to-gravitee.md): Migrate an API estate from a legacy gateway to Gravitee API Management with the migration agent skill. Follow the steps to convert and reconcile it.
- [Source gateway reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/migrate/source-gateway-reference.md): Source gateway migration reference for Gravitee API Management, covering Management API paths, Expression Language syntax, and credential export.
- [Deploy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/deploy.md): What to expect from the API Management Gateway under load before you run APIs on it.
- [Gateway benchmarks](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/deploy/gateway-benchmarks.md): Release-over-release performance results for the API Management Gateway on sync proxy APIs, with the scenarios and controlled environment used to produce them.
- [Manage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage.md): Open the Manage group of the API Management sidebar and choose API Proxies or API Products.
- [API Proxies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies.md): Create an API proxy, then configure it from the groups of its own sidebar. Choose the group that holds the setting you need.
- [Create an API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/create-an-api-proxy.md): Create an API proxy in the Gamma console with the from-scratch wizard or a quick-start template. Follow the steps through each wizard option.
- [Configure your API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/configure-your-api-proxy.md): Refine an API proxy from the detail workspace, which groups configuration into seven sidebar sections. Start with the setting you need to change.
- [General](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general.md): The General group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Overview, General, API Properties, Resources, Notifications, API Score, Response Templates, and CORS pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage general settings](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/manage-general-settings.md): Edit the name, version, metadata, and images of an API proxy, and start, stop, or delete it. Follow the steps on the General page to update them.
- [Configure API properties](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-api-properties.md): Define static or dynamic key/value properties that policies read at runtime through the Expression Language. Follow the steps to add and encrypt them.
- [Configure API resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-api-resources.md): Create API-level resources such as caches and OAuth providers that this API's policies reference at runtime. Follow the steps to add and manage them.
- [Configure notifications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-notifications.md): Send console, email, or webhook alerts when API events occur, such as a deployment or a subscription change. Follow the steps to add a notification.
- [Configure CORS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/general/configure-cors.md): Let browsers on other origins call your API by enabling cross-origin resource sharing and setting the allowed origins. Follow the steps to configure it.
- [Gateway](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway.md): The Gateway group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Entrypoints, Endpoints, Failover, Health Check Dashboard, and Reporter Settings pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure entrypoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-entrypoints.md): Change the context paths consumers use to reach an API proxy, or switch to virtual hosts. Follow the steps on the Entrypoints page to update them.
- [Configure endpoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-backend-security.md): Endpoint groups define where the API Gateway routes requests, with load balancing, timeouts, and TLS. Follow the steps to create a group and add endpoints.
- [Configure failover](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-failover.md): Turn on automatic retries with a circuit breaker so a slow or failing backend doesn't take your API down. Follow the steps to tune the thresholds.
- [Monitor endpoint health](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/monitor-endpoint-health.md): Track backend endpoint availability, response times, and failed health checks for an API. Follow the steps to open the Health Check Dashboard.
- [Configure logging and tracing](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/gateway/configure-logging-and-tracing.md): Control which request and response data an API proxy reports to logs and analytics, and turn on OpenTelemetry tracing. Follow the steps to configure it.
- [Design](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design.md): The Design group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Policy Studio pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Apply security policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/apply-security-policies.md): Policies are rules the API Gateway evaluates on every request and response, on top of security plans. Learn how the policy chain runs and how to apply one.
- [Control how policy flows are matched to requests](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/configure-flow-execution.md): Choose whether the API Gateway runs every matching policy flow or only the closest match. Follow the steps to set the flow execution mode.
- [Reuse policies with shared policy groups](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/design/shared-policy-groups.md): A shared policy group configures a set of policies once and reuses them across APIs and flows. Follow the steps to attach one in the Policy Studio.
- [Consumer Access](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access.md): The Consumer Access group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Plans, Consumers, and Broadcasts pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Secure your API proxy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/secure-your-api-proxy.md): Attach a Keyless, API Key, JWT, OAuth2, or mTLS plan to control how consumers authenticate to an API proxy. Follow the steps to add one and deploy it.
- [Establish consumer access](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/establish-consumer-access.md): Applications, subscriptions, and API keys control how consumers discover and authenticate with your API. Learn how to review and approve a request.
- [Broadcast messages to consumers](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/consumer-access/broadcast-messages-to-consumers.md): Send a one-way announcement to the consumers of an API proxy about a change, an update, or a maintenance window. Follow the steps to compose one.
- [Security](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/security.md): The Security group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Authorization and User Permissions pages. Choose the page you need.

- [Monitor](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/monitoring.md): The Monitoring group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Alerts and Audit Logs pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Review audit logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/monitoring/review-audit-logs.md): Trace who changed what, and when, on an API proxy with its audit trail. Follow the steps to read and filter the events on the Audit Logs page.
- [Operate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations.md): The Operations group of an API proxy's sidebar holds the Deployment pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations/configure-deployment.md): Choose which API Gateway instances load an API definition by assigning sharding tags. Follow the steps on the Deployment Configuration page.
- [Review deployment history](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-proxies/operations/review-deployment-history.md): Compare two deployed versions of an API definition and roll back to an earlier one. Follow the steps to inspect the history and restore a version.
- [API Products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products.md): Create an API Product, then configure it from the groups of its own sidebar. Choose the group that holds the setting you need.
- [Create API Products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/api-products.md): API Products bundle multiple API proxies into one consumer-facing product with shared plans. Learn when to use them and how to create one.
- [Configure API products](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/configure-your-api-product.md): Attach APIs, create plans, manage consumers and team access, and choose where an API Product deploys. Start with the area you need to configure.

- [Manage product APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/general/manage-product-apis.md): Attach and detach API proxies from an API Product so subscribers reach every API it bundles. Follow the steps to manage them on the APIs tab.
- [Security](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/security.md): The Security group of an API Product's sidebar holds the User Permissions pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Manage members and ownership](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/security/manage-members-and-ownership.md): Add members, attach groups, change roles, and transfer ownership of an API product. Follow the steps to manage access from the User Permissions tab.
- [Operate](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/operations.md): The Operations group of an API Product's sidebar holds the Deployment pages. Choose the page you need.
- [Configure product deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/operations/configure-product-deployment.md): Assign sharding tags to an API Product so only the API Gateway instances that advertise a matching tag load it. Follow the steps on the Deployment tab.
- [Manage API products with the Management API](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/manage-api-products-with-the-management-api.md): Create an API product, attach APIs, add and publish plans, and deploy it over the v2 Management API. Follow the full lifecycle with request examples.
- [API product configuration reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/manage/api-products/api-product-configuration-reference.md): Every API product property, the flag that decides which APIs can be bundled, and the plan types a product supports. Browse the full reference.
- [Observe](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/observability.md): The Observability group of the API Management sidebar holds the Dashboards, Logs, and Tracing pages. Open the view you need.
- [Dashboard and metrics](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/observability/api-dashboard.md): The Gamma homepage dashboard shows per-module metrics, quick actions, and guidance for empty states. Learn what each metric and quick action does.
- [Monitor API usage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/observability/monitor-api-usage.md): Track request volume, latency distribution, error rates, and top consumers for an API proxy. Follow the steps to open the observability dashboard.
- [View API logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/api-management/observability/view-api-logs.md): Inspect individual request logs and full request traces for an API proxy in the Trace Explorer. Follow the steps to open logs and traces.

- [Register your Kafka clusters](https://documentation.gravitee.io/event-stream-management/manage/register-your-kafka-clusters.md): Register an existing Kafka cluster with Gamma so Kafka Services and Virtual Clusters can be built on it. Follow the steps to register and deploy one.

- [Create a Kafka service with a registered cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/event-stream-management/build/create-a-kafka-service-with-a-registered-cluster.md): Create a Kafka Service bound to a connection on a registered Kafka cluster, with security plans and policies. - [Create a Kafka service with a Virtual Cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/event-stream-management/build/create-a-kafka-service-with-a-virtual-cluster.md): Create a Kafka Service backed by a Virtual Cluster to govern a federated multi-cluster Kafka environment. - [Establish a Virtual Cluster](https://documentation.gravitee.io/event-stream-management/build/establish-a-virtual-cluster.md): Federate multiple Kafka backends into one endpoint by establishing a Virtual Cluster. Follow the steps to choose a topology and compose the backends.

## Authorization Management

- [Get started](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/get-started.md): Authorization Management in Gamma, from the product overview to your first user, first imported resource, and first policy. Start with the overview.
- [Authorization Management overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/get-started/authorization-management-overview.md): Authorization Management enforces fine-grained, catalog-aware access control across API, MCP, AI model, and agent traffic. Learn how the pieces connect.
- [Create your first user](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/get-started/create-your-first-user.md): Create a local principal in Authorization Management so the policy engine has an identity to evaluate. Follow the steps to add your first one.
- [Import your first resource from Agent catalog](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/get-started/import-your-first-resource-from-agent-catalog.md): Import a resource from the AI Catalog so every policy refers to one canonical Entity ID. Follow the quickstart to import your first item.
- [Create your first policy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/get-started/create-your-first-policy.md): Create and deploy your first authorization policy in the visual editor to get a permit rule enforced at runtime. Follow the steps to build one.
- [Policy Structure](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage.md): The Policy Structure group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the entities, actions, and schemas your policies are written against.
- [Manage principals](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/principals.md): Sync principals from Access Management, create local ones, and give them attributes and relationships. Start with how your identities arrive.
- [Sync principals from Access Management](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/principals/sync-principals-from-access-management.md): Synchronize users and groups from Gravitee Access Management so policies can reference your identity directory. Follow the steps to run a sync.
- [Create a local principal](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/principals/create-a-local-principal.md): Create a principal directly in Authorization Management when the identity is not synced or imported. Follow the steps on the Entities page.
- [Add attributes to principals](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/principals/add-attributes-to-principals.md): Attach custom metadata to a principal, such as department or device trust, so policies test more than identity. Follow the steps to add attributes.
- [Build principal relationships](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/principals/build-principal-relationships.md): Link principals into parent-child hierarchies so that one policy can match every user in a group. Follow the steps to set and edit parents.
- [Manage resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/resources.md): Import resources from the Catalog, create local ones, and give them attributes and relationships. Start with where your resources come from.
- [Import resources from Catalog](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/resources/import-resources-from-catalog.md): Import resource entities from the AI Catalog into Authorization Management across all three categories. Follow the steps to browse and select them.
- [Create a local resource](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/resources/create-a-local-resource.md): Create a resource entity for the internal applications, data assets, and custom tools the AI Catalog does not hold. Follow the steps to add one.
- [Add attributes to resources](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/resources/add-attributes-to-resources.md): Attach custom metadata to a resource, such as sensitivity or ownership, so policy conditions can test it. Follow the steps to add attributes.
- [Build resource relationships](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/resources/build-resource-relationships.md): Establish parent-child hierarchies between resources so one policy covers a server and all of its tools. Follow the steps to set parents.
- [Manage actions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/actions.md): Actions are the verbs your policies grant or forbid. Create one, and see how action Entity IDs are named and scoped. Start by creating an action.
- [Create an action](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/actions/create-an-action.md): Define the verbs your policies grant or forbid by creating an action entity that statements reference. Follow the steps on the Actions page.
- [Action naming and scope](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/actions/action-naming-and-scope.md): How action Entity IDs are structured and how the policy engine resolves them against policy types and resource scopes. Browse the naming reference.
- [Manage schemas](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/schemas.md): The schema declares the entity types, relationships, and actions your policies are written against. Start with how a schema is generated.
- [Schema generation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/schemas/schema-generation.md): The schema is the contract your policies are written against, declaring entity types, relationships, and actions. Learn how it is generated and read.
- [Edit your schema](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/schemas/edit-your-schema.md): Modify an existing schema to add entity types, attributes, relationships, or actions. Follow the steps to edit, validate, and save your changes.
- [Validate schema changes](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/manage/schemas/validate-schema-changes.md): How the console validates a schema change before saving, and the errors to expect. Learn the validation pipeline and how to fix a failure.
- [Manage policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure.md): The Policy Management group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the policies for MCPs, AI models, APIs, A2A agents, and custom resources.
- [Create, update, and delete policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/create-update-delete-policies.md): Reference for the Authorization Management policy editor, covering how to create, name, build, deploy, and delete a policy. Browse the full reference.
- [MCP policy examples](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/mcp-policy-examples.md): Govern MCP servers, tools, and prompts with policy examples for group access, time windows, and device trust. Adapt an example to your own servers.
- [AI policy example](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/ai-policy-example.md): Control which teams reach which AI models with policy examples for token budgets, cost ceilings, and guardrails. Adapt an example to your own models.
- [API policy examples](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/api-policy-examples.md): Govern API proxies, endpoints, and data fields with policy examples for scopes, rate limits, and tenant isolation. Adapt an example to your own APIs.
- [Custom policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies.md): Custom policies govern resources outside the MCP, API, Agent, AI Model, and Event categories. Start with the overview, then create your first one.
- [Custom policies overview](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies/custom-policies-overview.md): Custom policies protect internal applications, data assets, and bespoke resources. Learn how they differ from service policies and when to use them.
- [Create a custom policy](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/configure/custom-policies/create-a-custom-policy.md): Create an authorization policy for resources not routed through the MCP, API, Agent, AI Model, or Event categories. Follow the steps to build one.
- [PDP Gateways](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate.md): The PDP Gateways group of the Authorization Management sidebar holds the gateways that evaluate your policies and how policies reach them.
- [Configure the Gravitee Gateway as a runtime](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate/configure-gravitee-gateway-as-runtime.md): Register a Gravitee Gateway as a Policy Decision Point so authorization decisions are evaluated at the wire level. Follow the steps to register one.
- [Configure a sidecar as runtime](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate/configure-sidecar-as-runtime.md): Deploy the Policy Decision Point as a sidecar beside your application for latency-sensitive or air-gapped environments. Follow the steps to deploy it.
- [Policy syncs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate/policy-syncs.md): How authorization policies travel from the control plane to your runtime gateways, and how long it takes. Learn to read and monitor sync status.
- [Analytics sent to the control plane](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate/analytics-sent-to-control-plane.md): The observability data that Policy Decision Point gateways send back to the Gravitee control plane. Learn what is collected and where it appears.
- [Authorization policy synchronization](https://documentation.gravitee.io/authorization-management/evaluate/authz-gateway-sync.md): Gamma Authorization Management evaluates policies locally inside the API Gateway. Learn how a change in the console becomes enforceable.

Learn how to configure it.

- [Console UI changes for multi-tenant Kafka endpoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/console_ui_changes_for_multi-tenant_kafka_endpoints.md): The API Management 4.12 Console endpoint table shows a Tenants column for multi-tenant Kafka endpoints. Learn what changed in the UI.
- [Enable OpenTelemetry Tracing for a Kafka API](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/enable-opentelemetry-tracing-for-a-kafka-api.md): Enable OpenTelemetry tracing for a single Kafka native API in API Management 4.12 once gateway tracing is in place. Follow the steps.
- [Entrypoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/entrypoints.md): Entrypoints define the protocol and settings consumers use to reach a Kafka API in API Management 4.12. Learn how to configure them.
- [Endpoints](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/endpoints.md): Endpoints define the protocol and settings a Kafka API uses to reach its backend in API Management 4.12. Learn how to configure them.
- [mTLS plans for Kafka native APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/mtls-plans.md): mTLS plans authenticate Kafka native API clients with X.509 certificates in API Management 4.12. Learn how subscriptions resolve.
- [Policies](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/policies.md): Policies are customizable rules the Gateway runs during a Kafka API transaction in API Management 4.12. Learn how they change behaviour.
- [Consumers](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/consumers.md): The Consumers section manages how a Kafka API is consumed in API Management 4.12, through plans and subscriptions. Learn what it covers.
- [Documentation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/documentation.md): The Documentation section creates Developer Portal pages for a Kafka API in API Management 4.12. Learn how to inform consumers.
- [Deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-apis/configure-kafka-apis/deployment.md): The Deployment section manages sharding tags and tracks changes to a Kafka API in API Management 4.12. Learn what it records.
- [Create and Configure Kafka Clusters](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/create-and-configure-kafka-clusters.md): Create and manage Kafka clusters from the Kafka UI in the API Management 4.12 Console. Follow the steps to configure a cluster.
- [Virtual Cluster Broker Addressing](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/virtual-cluster-broker-addressing.md): A Virtual Cluster presents several backend Kafka clusters as one in API Management 4.12. Learn how broker addressing works.
- [Configure and Deploy Kafka Console](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/configure-and-deploy-kafka-console.md): The Kafka Console is a standalone UI that integrates with API Management 4.12 to manage Kafka clusters. Follow the steps to deploy it.
- [Plans](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/plans.md): A plan is the service and access layer over a Kafka API in API Management 4.12, setting limits and validation. Learn the options.
- [Applications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/applications.md): Consumers register an application and subscribe to a plan to reach Kafka APIs in API Management 4.12. Learn what applications do.
- [Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/subscriptions.md): A subscription is the contract between a Kafka API publisher and consumer in API Management 4.12. Learn how one is created.
- [Other Ways Gravitee Supports Kafka](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/kafka-gateway/other-ways-gravitee-supports-kafka.md): Gravitee connects to Kafka through protocol mediation or native Kafka APIs in API Management 4.12. Compare the two approaches.

* [Resource Filtering](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/resource-filtering.md): The Resource Filtering policy restricts or allows access to specific REST resources in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure it.
* [REST to SOAP](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/rest-to-soap.md): The REST to SOAP policy exposes a SOAP backend as a REST API through API Management 4.11. Learn how the SOAP envelope is passed on.
* [Retry](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/retry.md): The Retry policy replays requests in API Management 4.11 when the backend fails or a condition matches. Learn how to configure it.
* [Role-based Access Control (RBAC)](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/role-based-access-control-rbac.md): The Role-based Access Control policy restricts a resource to the roles you specify in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure it.
* [SSL Enforcement](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/ssl-enforcement.md): The SSL Enforcement policy restricts access to requests meeting your SSL criteria in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure it.
* [Status Code Transformation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/status-code-transformation.md): The Status Code Transformation policy rewrites the upstream response status code in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure it.
* [Template](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/template.md): A policy template to fork as a quick starter for your own API Management 4.11 policy. Learn what the skeleton gives you to build on.
* [Traffic Shadowing](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/traffic-shadowing.md): The Traffic Shadowing policy asynchronously copies API Management 4.11 traffic to another service. Learn how to configure the copy.
* [Transform Headers](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/transform-headers.md): The Transform Headers policy overrides headers on incoming or outgoing API Management 4.11 traffic. Learn how to configure it.
* [Transform Query Parameters](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/transform-query-parameters.md): The Transform Query Parameters policy overrides incoming query parameters in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure the override.
* [URL Rewriting](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/url-rewriting.md): The URL Rewriting policy rewrites URLs in an API Management 4.11 response header or response body. Learn how to configure it.
* [WS Security Authentication](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/ws-security-authentication.md): The WS Security Authentication policy secures SOAP API calls in API Management 4.11 by comparing credentials. Learn how to configure it.
* [WS Security Sign](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/ws-security-sign.md): The WS Security Sign policy signs SOAP messages in API Management 4.11 to guarantee integrity. Learn how to configure the signature.
* [XML to JSON](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-to-json.md): The XML to JSON policy transforms XML content into JSON content in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure the transformation.
* [XML Threat Protection](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-threat-protection.md): The XML Threat Protection policy validates an XML request body against structural limits in API Management 4.11. Learn how to set them.
* [XML Validation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-validation.md): The XML Validation policy validates XML against an XSD schema in API Management 4.11, rejecting bad requests. Learn how to configure it.
* [XSLT](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xslt.md): The XSLT policy applies an XSL transformation to an XML request or response body in API Management 4.11. Learn how to configure it.
* [Webhook Signature Generator](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/webhook-signature-generator.md): The Webhook Signature Generator policy computes an HMAC signature over the response body in API Management 4.11. Learn how to set it.
* [Secure & Expose APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis.md): Plans, applications, and subscriptions govern how an API Management 4.11 API is exposed to consumers. Learn how the three fit together.
* [Plans](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans.md): A plan is the service and access layer an API Management 4.11 API needs before you expose it. Compare the plan types available.
* [Keyless](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/keyless.md): A keyless plan allows public access to an API Management 4.11 API with no authentication. Learn what it offers and when to use it.
* [API Key](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/api-key.md): The API Key plan verifies keys during request processing so only approved applications reach your API Management 4.11 API. Learn how.
* [OAuth2](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/oauth2.md): An OAuth 2.0 plan gives client applications secure, delegated access to an API Management 4.11 API. Learn how the standard works.
* [JWT](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/jwt.md): A JWT plan secures an API Management 4.11 API with signed claims passed between two parties. Learn how the token is validated.
* [Push](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/push.md): A Push plan suits an API Management 4.11 API with an entrypoint that sends message payloads, such as Webhook. Learn when to use it.
* [mTLS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/mtls.md): An mTLS plan requires a client certificate when connecting to an API Management 4.11 API. Learn how the certificate is used.
* [Applications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/applications.md): Consumers register an application and subscribe to a published plan to reach API Management 4.11 APIs. Learn what applications do.
* [Create an Application](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/applications/create-an-application.md): Create an application in the API Management 4.11 Console so a consumer can subscribe to a plan. Follow the steps to set one up.
* [Global Settings](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/applications/global-settings.md): An application's global settings hold its details and a danger zone for destructive actions in API Management 4.11. Learn what each does.

* [Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/secure-and-expose-apis/subscriptions.md): A subscription is how an API Management 4.11 publisher controls who uses their API and on what terms. Learn how subscriptions work.
