* [Reference Secrets in APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/prepare-a-production-environment/sensitive-data-management/api-secrets/reference-secrets-in-apis.md): Reference secret manager values from within an API Management 4.11 API to hide sensitive data. Follow the steps to use them.
* [Plugin support](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/prepare-a-production-environment/sensitive-data-management/plugin-support.md): Which API Management 4.11 plugins support referencing secrets. Browse the support table before you configure a secret in one.
* [Plugins](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins.md): Plugins expand what the API Management 4.11 Gateway, Management API, and other components can do. Compare the types of plugin available.
* [Deployment](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins/deployment.md): Deploy an API Management 4.11 plugin by copying its archive into the plugins directory. Follow the steps, including for air-gapped clusters.
* [Customization](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins/customization.md): API Management 4.11 plugins extend and customize component behaviour. Learn the common structure a plugin shares before you build one.
* [Context-Aware Logging in Plugins](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins/customization/context-aware-logging.md): Use the context-aware logging API when developing API Management 4.11 Gateway plugins. Follow the steps for request-scoped logging.
* [Policies for the Entrypoint Connect phase](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins/customization/entrypoint-connect-phase-policies.md): The Entrypoint Connect phase runs when a client opens a native Kafka connection to the API Management 4.11 Gateway. Learn how to interrupt one.
* [Enforce Logging Rules with ArchUnit Maven Plugin](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/plugins/customization/archunit-maven-plugin.md): The ArchUnit Maven plugin enforces context-aware logging rules at build time for API Management 4.11 plugins. Follow the steps to configure it.
* [Gravitee Expression Language](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/gravitee-expression-language.md): Gravitee Expression Language queries object graphs to configure API Management 4.11 policies dynamically. Learn the syntax and expression markers.
* [Management API Reference](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/management-api-reference.md): The Gravitee Management API is the programmatic interface to the API Management 4.11 backend. Browse the reference for its two sub-APIs.
* [Expose APIM as an MCP Server](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.11/expose-apim-as-an-mcp-server.md): Install the API Management 4.11 MCP Server so AI clients can call your platform. Follow the steps to install it and create a service account token.

- [JWS Validator](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/jws-validator.md): The JWS Validator policy checks token signature, certificate, and expiry before API Management 4.10 calls the backend. Learn how to set it.
- [JWT Validator](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/4.9-jwt-validator.md): The JWT policy validates a token's signature and expiry before API Management 4.10 calls the backend. Learn how to configure it.
- [Kafka ACL](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-acl.md): The Kafka ACL policy defines access control lists on cluster resources proxied by API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure them.
- [Kafka Message Filtering](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-message-filtering.md): The Kafka Message Filtering policy controls which Kafka messages reach consumers through API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure it.
- [Kafka Offloading](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-offloading.md): The Kafka Offloading policy controls how message content is offloaded to storage in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure it.
- [Kafka Quota](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-quota.md): The Kafka Quota policy enforces limits on the data Kafka clients exchange through API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure quotas.
- [Kafka Topic Mapping](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-topic-mapping.md): The Kafka Topic Mapping policy maps one topic name to another so clients can use their own in API Management 4.10. Learn how to set it.
- [Kafka Transform Key](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/kafka-transform-key.md): The Kafka Transform Key policy adds a custom message key in API Management 4.10 so you can control partitioning. Learn how to set it.
- [Keyless](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/keyless.md): The Keyless security policy treats every request to an API Management 4.10 API as valid and blocks none. Learn when to use it.
- [Latency](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/latency.md): The Latency policy adds artificial delay to a request or a response in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure the delay.
- [Maven Archetype](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/maven-archetype.md): A policy is a step in the API Management 4.10 request and response proxy chain. Learn how to generate one with the Maven archetype.
- [Message Filtering](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/message-filtering.md): The Message Filtering policy lets a publisher control which messages are streamed to subscribers in API Management 4.10. Learn how to set it.
- [Metrics Reporter](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/metrics-reporter.md): The Metrics Reporter policy pushes request metrics to a custom endpoint from API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure the reporter.
- [Mock](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/mock.md): The Mock policy returns mock responses from API Management 4.10 so you can test without a backend. Learn how to configure the mock.
- [mTLS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/mtls.md): The mTLS policy verifies that a client certificate is present on an API Management 4.10 request. Learn how to configure the policy.
- [OAS Validation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/oas-validation.md): The OAS Validation policy checks requests and responses against an embedded OpenAPI specification in API Management 4.10. Learn how.
- [OAuth2](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/oauth2.md): The OAuth2 policy checks access token validity through introspection in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure the policy.

Learn how to configure it.

- [Rate Limit](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/rate-limit.md): API Management 4.10 offers three rate limit policies for capping request volume. Compare them and learn how to configure each one.
- [Regex Threat Protection](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/regex-threat-protection.md): The Regex Threat Protection policy inspects request headers, path, parameters, and body in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure it.
- [Request Content Limit](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/request-content-limit.md): The Request Content Limit policy caps the request content length API Management 4.10 accepts. Learn how to configure the maximum.
- [Request Validation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/request-validation.md): The Request Validation policy validates incoming HTTP requests against rules in API Management 4.10. Learn how to define the rules.
- [Resource Filtering](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/resource-filtering.md): The Resource Filtering policy restricts or allows access to specific REST resources in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure it.
- [REST to SOAP](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/rest-to-soap.md): The REST to SOAP policy exposes a SOAP backend as a REST API through API Management 4.10. Learn how the SOAP envelope is passed on.
- [Retry](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/retry.md): The Retry policy replays requests in API Management 4.10 when the backend fails or a condition matches. Learn how to configure it.

Learn how to configure it.
- [SSL Enforcement](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/ssl-enforcement.md): The SSL Enforcement policy restricts access to requests meeting your SSL criteria in API Management 4.10. Learn how to configure it.

- [Plans](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans.md): A plan is the service and access layer an API Management 4.10 API needs before you expose it. Compare the plan types available.
- [Keyless](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/keyless.md): A keyless plan allows public access to an API Management 4.10 API with no authentication. Learn what it offers and when to use it.
- [API Key](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/api-key.md): The API Key plan verifies keys during request processing so only approved applications reach your API Management 4.10 API. Learn how.
- [OAuth2](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/oauth2.md): An OAuth 2.0 plan gives client applications secure, delegated access to an API Management 4.10 API. Learn how the standard works.
- [JWT](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/jwt.md): A JWT plan secures an API Management 4.10 API with signed claims passed between two parties. Learn how the token is validated.
- [Push](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/push.md): A Push plan suits an API Management 4.10 API with an entrypoint that sends message payloads, such as Webhook. Learn when to use it.
- [mTLS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.10/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/mtls.md): An mTLS plan requires a client certificate when connecting to an API Management 4.10 API. Learn how the certificate is used.

* [XML to JSON](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-to-json.md): The XML to JSON policy transforms XML content into JSON content in API Management 4.9. Learn how to configure the transformation.
* [XML Threat Protection](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-threat-protection.md): The XML Threat Protection policy validates an XML request body against structural limits in API Management 4.9. Learn how to set them.
* [XML Validation](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xml-validation.md): The XML Validation policy validates XML against an XSD schema in API Management 4.9, rejecting bad requests. Learn how to configure it.
* [XSLT](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/create-and-configure-apis/apply-policies/policy-reference/xslt.md): The XSLT policy applies an XSL transformation to an XML request or response body in API Management 4.9. Learn how to configure it.
* [Secure & Expose APIs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis.md): Plans, applications, and subscriptions govern how an API Management 4.9 API is exposed to consumers. Learn how the three fit together.
* [Plans](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans.md): A plan is the service and access layer an API Management 4.9 API needs before you expose it. Compare the plan types available.
* [Keyless](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/keyless.md): A keyless plan allows public access to an API Management 4.9 API with no authentication. Learn what it offers and when to use it.
* [API Key](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/api-key.md): The API Key plan verifies keys during request processing so only approved applications reach your API Management 4.9 API. Learn how.
* [OAuth2](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/oauth2.md): An OAuth 2.0 plan gives client applications secure, delegated access to an API Management 4.9 API. Learn how the standard works.
* [JWT](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/jwt.md): A JWT plan secures an API Management 4.9 API with signed claims passed between two parties. Learn how the token is validated.
* [Push](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/push.md): A Push plan suits an API Management 4.9 API with an entrypoint that sends message payloads, such as Webhook. Learn when to use it.
* [mTLS](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/plans/mtls.md): An mTLS plan requires a client certificate when connecting to an API Management 4.9 API. Learn how the certificate is used.
* [Applications](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/applications.md): Consumers register an application and subscribe to a published plan to reach API Management 4.9 APIs. Learn what applications do.
* [Create an Application](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/applications/create-an-application.md): Create an application in the API Management 4.9 Console so a consumer can subscribe to a plan. Follow the steps to set one up.
* [Global Settings](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/applications/global-settings.md): An application's global settings hold its details and a danger zone for destructive actions in API Management 4.9. Learn what each does.
* [User and Group Access](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/applications/user-and-group-access.md): Manage user and group access to an individual API Management 4.9 application. Follow the steps to add and remove members.
* [Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/subscriptions.md): A subscription is how an API Management 4.9 publisher controls who uses their API and on what terms. Learn how subscriptions work.
* [Subscription Requests](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/subscriptions/subscription-requests.md): A subscription is a contract between an API Management 4.9 publisher and a consumer. Learn how requests are made and approved.
* [Manage Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/subscriptions/manage-subscriptions.md): Find and manage an application's subscriptions from the API Management 4.9 Console. Follow the steps to review and change them.
* [Transfer Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/secure-and-expose-apis/subscriptions/transfer-subscriptions.md): Transfer an active API Management 4.9 subscription to another plan with the same security type. Follow the steps to move it.
* [Developer Portal](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal.md): The Developer Portal 4.9 is the web application where consumers discover and consume your APIs. Learn what the portal offers.
* [New Developer Portal](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/new-developer-portal.md): The features and functionality of the New Developer Portal 4.9. Browse the tiles to find the area you want to configure.

* [Layout and Theme](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/new-developer-portal/layout-and-theme.md): Set the layout and theme of the New Developer Portal 4.9, and browse the catalog by category. Learn what you can change.
* [Customize the homepage](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/new-developer-portal/customize-the-homepage.md): Customize the New Developer Portal 4.9 homepage with standard Markdown. Follow the steps to change what consumers see first.
* [Application Logs](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/new-developer-portal/application-logs.md): API subscribers view paginated logs for every API their application subscribes to in the Developer Portal 4.9. Learn how to read them.
* [Configure Webhook Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/new-developer-portal/configure-webhook-subscriptions.md): Configure a webhook subscription from the Developer Portal 4.9 catalog. Follow the steps to set it up for your application.

* [Portal Settings](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/classic-developer-portal/portal-settings.md): Reach the Developer Portal 4.9 settings from the Management Console sidebar. Follow the steps to open and change the settings.
* [Layout and Theme](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/classic-developer-portal/layout-and-theme.md): Administrators can change the layout and theme of the Developer Portal 4.9 to control how APIs are presented. Learn what you can customize.
* [Manage Users](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/classic-developer-portal/manage-users.md): Users reaching the Developer Portal 4.9 from the Management Console are signed in automatically. Learn how user management works.
* [Webhook Subscriptions](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/classic-developer-portal/webhook-subscriptions.md): API consumers configure their webhook subscriptions from the Developer Portal 4.9. Learn what the configuration involves for them.
* [Create an Application](https://documentation.gravitee.io/apim/4.9/developer-portal/classic-developer-portal/create-an-application.md): Unless an API has a keyless plan, a consumer registers an application in the Developer Portal 4.9 and subscribes. Follow the steps.
